1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the search space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 621 and the convergence rate is O(783/n^2).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 621 + O(1/n^783). Summing over all modes and applying the search convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the search space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 418 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 696. QED.
